2. Use alternating minimization from LP SOTA described. This might escape local minima better. Let's detail LP approach.

Then we can run mirror/gradient descent:
p = init sum1.
for iter:
  c=conv FFT p*p, S=1
  r=n*c  (since S=1) length 2n-1
  compute w = softmax(β*r) (stable)
  compute y=Hankel_product(p,w)
  g=2n*y
  # remove mean? To make zero-sum, subtract mean(g) maybe, because gradient under sum constraint: effective gradient g - mean(g) (with equal weights) for linear normalization. Do that to reduce drift.
  g = g - g.mean()
  p = p - lr * g
  p = clip(p,0)
  normalize sum1
  maintain momentum.

Let's design robust local optimizer with time budget and beta annealing:
```
def optimize_logsum(a, time_limit, beta_list, lr0=0.05, patience=...):
  n=len(a); a=normalize(a,n); best=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score(a)
  size=next_pow2(3n-3)
  for beta in beta_list:
    lr=lr0; v=zeros; it=0; phase_deadline=time.monotonic()+time_alloc
    no_improve=0
    while time<phase_deadline:
      a=clip normalize
      fa=rfft(a,size); c=irfft(fa*fa,size)[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
      M=c.max(); score=2*M/n
      if score < best_score: best...
      elif score > best_score*(1+1e-6): no_improve++
      # if no_improve high: reduce lr, maybe reset v; if phase ended break
      r = c / n
      # softmax w. If β large and r has many equal max? For flat optimum, w uniform over natters. Use stable.
      # To compute y_a: w_rev_fft. Instead of full w maybe if β large use selected top to reduce FFT of w? FFT of full w cheap but w mostly zeros? Need forward transform of w_rev length 2n-1 padded to size. We can reuse previous w_fft? w changes. Use np.fft.rfft(w_rev,size).
      w_rev = w[::-1]
      fw = rfft(w_rev,size)
      conv_aw = irfft(fa*fw,size)
      start=2*n-2; stop=n-2
      y_a = conv_aw[start:stop:-1]
      # y_a length? If stop n-3? Let's ensure.
```

      G = 2.0 * y_a
      G -= G.mean()
      # Gradient scaling adaptive:
      grange = G.max() - G.min()
      if grange <=1e-12: lr*=0.5? else adjust lr to keep update norm ~ update_target? Let's use target_update = 0.05 * n? No, a sum n. We want max change per iter maybe 0.1 (relative to a ~1). Adaptive beta and step details:
```
def optimize_logsum(a, deadline, rng, initial_beta=10, max_beta=300, factor_time=...):
  n = len(a)
  a = normalize(a,n)
  best_a=a.copy(); best_score=fast_score_exact_fft(a)
  size = next_pow2(3*n-2)
  beta = initial_beta
  lr = 0.02
  v = zeros
  improve_count=0
  # We'll allocate time slots: while time<deadline:
  # each phase with same beta for at most phase_time = min(60, remaining*0.05)
  phase_start = time.monotonic(); phase_score_best=best_score; phase_stage=0
  while time < deadline:
     if time - phase_start > phase_duration:
        # increase beta or adjust
        beta = min(beta*2, 500)
        phase_start=time...
        lr = 0.02; v=zeros*0.5
     ... updates
```

Let's implement combined objective:
```
def gradient_combined(p, c, beta, lambda_sq=1.0):
  r = n*c
  # logsum weights
  z = beta*(r - r.max())
  w = exp(z); wsum=w.sum(); if wsum>0: w/=wsum else w argmax
  eps = 0.02
  w_log = (1-eps)*w + eps/len(r)
  y_log = hankel(p, w_log)
  g_log = 2*n*y_log
  # square weights centered maybe r - r.mean() to not push mean? But mean fixed; use r_mean = r - r.mean() (sum ~0). If use r, gradient includes increasing all r if mean not fixed due numerical; better use r_mean.

def optimize_combined(a, deadline, rng, beta0=8, beta_max=200, lam_sq=5.0, momentum=0.85, step=0.08, print_interval=...):
  n=len(a); a=normalize(a,n)
  p=a/n? Actually easier maintain p sum1. a for final? p.
  p = a / a.sum()
  logp = log(p+1e-300); logp -= logp.max()
  v=zeros
  best_p=p; best_score=score_from_p(p)
  size=next_pow2(3n-2)
  beta=beta0; lam=lam_sq; step=step0; it=0; no_improve=0; phase_start=...
  while time<deadline:
     beta maybe increase if no_improve or schedule: if it % 20000 ==0: beta=min(beta*1.3,beta_max); lam*=?; v*=0.5
     p = softmax(logp) # with max
     # if p sum issue: p=clip
     fa=rfft(p,size)
     c=irfft(fa*fa,size)[:2n-1]; c=np.maximum(c,0)
     r = n*c
     score=2*r.max()
     update best
     # weights
     z = beta*(r - r.max()); w=exp(z); wsum; if wsum>0: w/=wsum else ...
     eps=0.03; w_log=(1-eps)*w + eps/r.size
     y_log=hankel(p, w_log, fa, size)
     g_log=2*n*y_log
     w_sq = r - r.mean()
     y_sq=hankel(p, w_sq, fa, size) # can compute with same fa and fw_sq
     g_sq=(4.0*n/len(r))*y_sq
     g = g_log + lam*g_sq
     h = g - g.mean()
     hmax = np.abs(h).max()
     if hmax < 1e-12:
        # random kick?
        break phase
     h_norm = h/hmax
     # adapt step maybe based on recent score. Use step=base/(1+?); base 0.1
     v = momentum*v + h_norm  # maybe with step later
     v /= max(1.0, np.abs(v).max())? Might clip.
     p = softmax(logp - step * v)
     # if score improves: step*=1.02; no=0 else no++
     # if no>... step*=0.7; v*=0.5; if step small break? If frequently worsen >?), reset logp from best
```

Need exact `fast_score_p(p,n)`:
```
def fast_score_p(p, n):
    size=1; while size<2*n-1: size*=2
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,size)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max()), c
```
For many candidate evaluation, recompute size each. Could cache? top-level helper no closures; pass size.

In optimizer, precompute size. Need `hankel_product(p, weights, fp, size, n)`:
```
def hankel_product(p, weights, fp, size, n):
    # weights length 2n-1
    wr = weights[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, size)
    return conv[2*n-2 : n-2 : -1].copy()
```
This returns y = ∑ p_i weights_{i+j}. If p is array length n. For p maybe exactly sum1.

But note `fp` is rfft of p with size. We compute c using fp*fp. In same optimizer, after p update, we recompute fp. For weights r, r from c computed from p; can compute fw_sq each iter.

For combined gradient, maybe computing both y_log and y_sq requires two FFTs of weights (w_log and w_sq). We can combine weights into one vector to compute one Hankel? Since gradient is linear combination of Hankel products: g = A*Hankel(p,w1)+B*Hankel(p,w2) = Hankel(p, A w1 + B w2) (linearity, if scales constant). Yes! We can combine weights before FFT: `w_combined = (2*n) * w_log + lam * (4*n/L) * (r - mean)`. Then `y=Hankel(p, w_combined)`, `g=y`. Great, reduces one FFT. Because H linear. Let's verify: y_w=∑w_k p_{k-i}. The combined gradient uses sum with coefficients; same. So only one weight FFT per iter. Nice. Define:
```

y = hankel(p, w_combined)
g = y  # since weights already include gradient coefficients
```

Pseudo detailed:
```
def optimize_smooth(p0, deadline, rng, beta_start=4.0, beta_end=250.0, lam_sq=1.0, lam_hinge=0.8, momentum=0.85, step0=0.08):
    n=len(p0)
    p = np.asarray(p0,float)
    p = np.maximum(p,0)
    S=p.sum()
    if S<1e-300: p=np.ones(n)/n
    else p=p/S
    size=1; while size<3*n-2: size*=2
    L=2*n-1
    logp=np.log(p+1e-300); logp-=logp.max()
    best_p=p.copy(); best_score=fast_score_p(p,n)[0]
    best_logp=logp.copy()
    v=np.zeros(n)
    beta=beta_start
    lam_sq=...; lam_hinge=...
    step=step0
    no_improve=0
    it=0
    phase_cycles=0
    start=time.monotonic()
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
       # update beta based on no_improve or cycles
       if it % 5000 == 0 and it>0:
           beta = min(beta*1.5, beta_end)
           # reset step/lr maybe
           v *= 0.5
           # occasionally random kick if no_improve high
       p = np.exp(logp - logp.max())
       p /= p.sum()
       fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
       c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,size)
       c=c[:L]
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
       r=n*c
       score=float(2.0*r.max())
       if not finite: reinit best; break
       if score < best_score - 1e-12:
           best_score=score; best_p=p.copy(); best_logp=logp.copy(); no_improve=0; step=min(step*1.05,0.25)
       else:
           no_improve += 1
           if no_improve > 10000:
               step*=0.7; v*=0.4; no_improve=0
              if step < 1e-4: break phase? maybe random restart from best
       # weights
       z=beta*(r - r.max())
       # if beta large, underflow: w zero except max. stable.
       w=np.exp(z - z.max())? z already rmax. wsum.
       if wsum ==0 or not finite:
           w= zeros; w[argmax]=1
       else w/=wsum
       eps=0.02; w_log=(1-eps)*w + eps/L
       # square centered
       rm = r - r.mean()
       rm_max = np.abs(rm).max()
       if rm_max > 1e-12: w_sq=rm/rm_max else zeros
       tau=0.75
       ex = r - tau
       ex = np.maximum(ex,0)
       ex_max=ex.max()
       w_hinge = ex/ex_max if ex_max>1e-12 else zeros
       w_comb = w_log + lam_sq*w_sq + lam_hinge*w_hinge
       # Could normalize w_comb by its max abs to prevent huge weights? Since h normalized, not necessary but numerical if lam large. Use w_comb / max(1, abs max?) maybe keep relative.
       # If w_comb all zero? h zero.
       y = hankel_product(p, w_comb, fp, size, n)
       g = y
       h = g - g.mean()
       hmax = np.abs(h).max()
       if hmax < 1e-14:
           # random kick
           kick = rng.normal(0,1,n); kick-=kick.mean(); p=softmax(logp+0.1*kick/hmax?)
           continue
       h_norm = h/hmax
       v = momentum*v + h_norm
       vmax = np.abs(v).max()
       if vmax > 1.0: v /= vmax
       # step adapt maybe if step too high: if logp has huge? normalized v<=1.
       logp = logp - step * v
       # prevent logp range too large? optional: if max-logp - min > 80, rescale subtract max (softmax does)
       it +=1
    return best_p, best_score
```

def generate_candidates(n, parent, rng, max_count=40):
  cands=[]
  if parent is not None:
     cands.append(resample(parent,n))
     # perturb parent
     for sigma in [0.05,0.2,0.5]: cands.append(perturb(p,sigma))
  x = (arange(n)+0.5)/n
  cands.append(uniform)
  for a in [0.2,0.5,0.8,1.2,2,4]: cands.append(normalize(exp(a*x))); cands.append(normalize(exp(-a*x)))
  for gamma in [0.3,0.5,0.7,1,1.5,2,3,5]: cands.append(normalize(x**gamma)); cands.append(normalize((1-x)**gamma)); cands.append(normalize(x**gamma*(1-x)**(gamma-0.5)? no)

  cands.extend(random_exp_fourier)
  # evaluate and keep top

Let's write helper functions carefully.

`resample_sequence(seq, n)`:
```
def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.clip(arr, 0.0, None)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0/n)
    if arr.size == n:
        out = arr.copy()
    else:
        old = arr.size
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(x1, x0, arr)
    out = np.maximum(out, 0.0)
    out = normalize_sequence(out, 1.0)
    return out
```
`np.interp` with old=1: linspace works? if old=1, x0 [0], x1... returns constant. normalize handles.

`normalize_sequence(arr, target)`:
```
arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
s = arr.sum()
if s <= 1e-300:
    return np.full(arr.shape, target/arr.size) if target>0 and arr.size>0 else arr
return arr * (target/s)
```
If target=1.

`next_power_of_two(x)` handle x<=1.

`fast_score_p(p,n)` size maybe next pow2 2n-1. If p sum not1? It assumes sum1; to be safe normalize? Avoid copy? Could compute S=p.sum(); score=2n*cmax/S^2. For candidate, p already normalized. For robust, use S.
```
def fast_score_p(p,n,size=None):
    p=np.asarray(p,float)
    S=p.sum()
    if S <=0: return np.inf
    if size is None: size=next_power_of_two(2*n-1)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p,size)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fp*fp,size)[:2*n-1]
    c=np.maximum(c,0)
    return float(2.0*n*c.max()/(S*S))
```
If p sum1, S=1. If not, correct.

`hankel_y(p, weights, fp, size, n)` expects p length n, weights len L. Return float array length n.
```
def hankel_y(p, weights, fp, size, n):
    wr = weights[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, size)
    start = 2*n - 2
    stop = n - 2
    return conv[start:stop:-1].copy()
```
Check stop for n=1: start=0, stop=-1, slice [0:-1:-1] empty. We won't use n small. For n=2: start=2, stop=0, slice [2:0:-1] -> indices2,1 length2. Good. For n=3 start=4 stop=1 ->4,3,2 length3.

```
for trial in range(20):
   m = int(rng.integers(4, 17))
   sig = np.zeros(P)
   for k in range(1,m+1):
       amp = np.abs(rng.standard_normal()) / (k ** 1.0) * 0.7
       phase = rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi)
       sig += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + phase)
   # maybe also linear term
   sig += rng.uniform(-1,1)*x
   f = np.exp(sig - sig.max())
   cands.append(normalize(f,1.0))
```

Let's detail `optimize_smooth` code:

```
def optimize_smooth(p0, deadline, rng, beta_start=3.0, beta_max=400.0, lam_sq=1.0, lam_hinge=1.0, momentum=0.85, step0=0.02, verbose=False):
    n = len(p0)
    p = np.asarray(p0, dtype=np.float64)
    p = normalize_sequence(p, 1.0)
    size = next_power_of_two(max(8, 3*n - 2))
    L = 2*n - 1
    logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    logp -= logp.max()
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_p(p, n)
    v = np.zeros(n)
    beta = float(beta_start)
    lam_sq_local = float(lam_sq)
    lam_hinge_local = float(lam_hinge)
    step = float(step0)
    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_adjust = time.monotonic()
    last_print = -100.0
    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = softmax_log(logp)  # top-level
        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, size)
        c = c[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        r = n * c
        cur_score = float(2.0 * r.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            best_score = fast_score_p(best_p,n)
            break
        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            step = min(0.08, step * 1.02)
            if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 30:
                print(...)
                last_print = time.monotonic()
        else:
            no_improve += 1
        # adjust every ~30s if no improve
        if time.monotonic() - last_adjust > 30.0 and no_improve > 1000:
            beta = min(beta * 1.4, beta_max)
            lam_sq_local = min(lam_sq_local + 0.1, 3.0)
            step *= 0.9
            v *= 0.5
            no_improve = 0
            last_adjust = time.monotonic()
            if no_improve... random? 
        # random restart if very stuck
        if it > 5000 and no_improve > 10000 and time.monotonic() > last_adjust + 60:
            sigma = 0.1 + 0.05*( (it//10000)%3 )
            logp = np.log(best_p + 1e-300)
            logp -= logp.max()
            logp = logp + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v = np.zeros(n)
            step = max(0.01, step)
            no_improve=0
            last_adjust=...
        # weights
        rmax = r.max()
        if not finite rmax: break? r finite.
        z = beta * (r - rmax)
        w = np.exp(z)
        wsum = w.sum()
        if not finite wsum or wsum <=0:
           w = np.zeros(L); w[int(np.argmax(r))] = 1.0
        else: w /= wsum
        eps=0.02
        w_log = (1.0 - eps) * w + eps / L
        rm = r - r.mean()
        rm_max = np.abs(rm).max()
        if rm_max > 1e-12: w_sq = rm / rm_max
        else: w_sq = np.zeros(L)
        tau=0.75
        ex = r - tau
        ex = np.maximum(ex, 0.0)
        ex_max = ex.max()
        if ex_max > 1e-12: w_h = ex / ex_max
        else: w_h = np.zeros(L)
        w_comb = w_log + lam_sq_local * w_sq + lam_hinge_local * w_h
        y = hankel_y(p, w_comb, fp, size, n)
        g = n * y
        h = g - g.mean()
        hmax = np.abs(h).max()
        if hmax <= 1e-12:
            # small random
            if it > 1000:
               noise = rng.normal(0,1,n); noise -= noise.mean(); logp += 0.1*noise; v=0
            else: break
        else:
            if hmax > 2.0:
                h = h / hmax
            v = momentum * v + h
            vmax = np.abs(v).max()
            if vmax > 5.0:
                v = v / vmax
            logp = logp - step * v
        it += 1
    # ensure best_p valid
    best_p = normalize_sequence(best_p,1.0)
    final_score = fast_score_p(best_p,n)
    if final_score < best_score:
       best_score=final_score; best_p=normalize...
    return best_p, best_score
```

`softmax_log(logp)` top-level:
```
def softmax_log(logp):
    m = np.max(logp)
    e = np.exp(logp - m)
    e = np.maximum(e, 0.0)
    s = e.sum()
    if s <= 0:
        n = len(e); return np.full(n,1.0/n), logp - m? 
    return e/s
```
If logp differences huge, exp underflows many to0, sum positive. If all underflow? max exp=1, sum≥1. Good.

Detailed `generate_preview_candidates`:
```
def generate_preview_candidates(P, parent, rng, extra):
    cands=[]
    cands.append(np.full(P, 1.0/P))
    x = (np.arange(P)+0.5)/P
    seqs=[]
    if parent is not None: seqs.append(parent)
    if extra is not None: seqs.append(extra)
    for seq in seqs:
       base=resample_sequence(seq,P)
       cands.append(base)
       for sigma in (0.08,0.25,0.6):
          noise=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,sigma,P))
          cands.append(normalize_sequence(base*noise,1.0))
    gammas=(0.3,0.5,0.8,1.0,1.5,2.0,3.0)
    for gamma in gammas:
       cands.append(normalize_sequence(x**gamma,1.0))
       cands.append(normalize_sequence((1.0-x)**gamma,1.0))
       cands.append(normalize_sequence(x**gamma*(1.0-x)**(0.5*gmod?) no)
    for a in (0.5,1.0,2.0,4.0,8.0):
       cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(a*x),1.0))
       cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(-a*x),1.0))
    for amp in (0.3,0.5,0.7,0.9):
       for phase in np.linspace(0.0,2*np.pi,8,endpoint=False):
          f=1.0 + amp*np.sin(2*np.pi*x + phase)
          cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f,0.0),1.0))
          f=1.0 + amp*np.sin(4*np.pi*x + phase) + 0.5*amp*np.sin(6*np.pi*x + phase*1.7)
          cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f,0.0),1.0))
          f=np.exp(0.5 + amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*x + phase) - x)
          cands.append(normalize_sequence(f,1.0))
    # random Fourier
    for _ in range(24):
       m=int(rng.integers(4,17))
       sig=np.zeros(P)
       for k in range(1,m+1):
          amp=abs(rng.standard_normal()) * 0.8 / (k**0.9)
          phase=rng.uniform(0.0,2*np.pi)
          sig += amp * np.cos(2*np.pi*k*x + phase)
       sig += rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0) * x
       f=np.exp(sig - np.max(sig))
       cands.append(normalize_sequence(f,1.0))
    if len(cands)>150: cands=cands[:150]
    return cands
```

def softmax_log(logp):
   logp=np.asarray(logp,float)
   if logp.size==0: return np.array([])
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(logp)): return np.full(logp.shape,1.0/logp.size)
   m=np.max(logp)
   e=np.exp(logp-m)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return full
   return e/s
```

One more thought: The optimizer objective in logp update uses gradient h of F(p), but softmax p=logsoftmax. The exact gradient w.r.t logp is p_i * (g_i - dot(p,g)). We are using h = g - mean(g), not p_i-weighted. This is not exact for logp, but a heuristic multiplicative gradient. Could it fail? It is common. If we want exact softmax gradient, use `u = p * (g - np.dot(p,g))`; then for p flat, u~g/n, small; need scale. We could use that with scaling n*u to O1. Maybe exactness improves. But heuristic allows small p changes. Let's consider using exact and scale: `h = n * p * (g - np.dot(p,g))`. For flat, p=1/n, dot=mean, h = g-mean, same as our h (since n*(1/n)*(g-mean)=g-mean). For nonuniform, exact gradient damps small p. Our h doesn't damp, increasing small coords more. Heuristic likely okay and maybe better for exploration. It's not mathematically gradient of F in logp but still descent? Not guaranteed. Could there be cycles? We adapt. Many MCMC-like multiplicative updates use p_i *= exp(-η g_i) heuristic. It works for nonnegative simplex.

If we want safer, we could combine: `h = n * (p * (g - np.dot(p,g)) + (1-p?)? )`. Simpler keep heuristic.

Alternative use additive update in `a` normalized; maybe better exact. But log update positivity. We'll trust.

Maybe add an occasional additive gradient step? Could overcomplicate.

```python
import numpy as np
import time


"""
Search for nonnegative coefficient sequences minimizing

    2 * n * max_k (a * a)_k / sum(a)^2

using:
  - several sequence lengths (4096, 8192, 16384)
  - smooth random/parametric initializations
  - a combined smoothed objective:
        log-sum-exp(r) + lambda * variance(r) + hinge(r - 0.75)
    where r_k = n * (a*a)_k for sum(a)=1
  - FFT-based exact gradients for the smooth objective, including a Hankel
    product so the gradient can be computed in O(n log n)
  - multiplicative logit updates with momentum
  - a final active-peak polishing phase for the true max objective
  - best-sequence tracking and optional exact validation
"""


def next_power_of_two(m):
    m = max(1, int(m))
    p = 1
    while p < m:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def normalize_sequence(arr, target=1.0):
    arr = np.asarray(arr, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return arr
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    s = float(arr.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300:
        if target > 0.0:
            return np.full(arr.shape, target / float(arr.size), dtype=np.float64)
        return arr
    return arr * (float(target) / s)


def softmax_log(logp):
    logp = np.asarray(logp, dtype=np.float64)
    n = logp.size
    if n == 0:
        return logp.copy()
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(logp)):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(logp))
    e = np.exp(logp - m)
    e = np.maximum(e, 0.0)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def fast_score_p(p, n, size=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    S = float(p.sum())
    if S <= 1e-300 or n <= 0:
        return float("inf")
    if size is None:
        size = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, size)
    c = c[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return float(2.0 * n * c.max() / (S * S))


def resample_sequence(seq, n):
    arr = np.asarray(seq, dtype=np.float64)
    arr = np.nan_to_num(arr, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
    arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
    n = int(n)
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == 0:
        return np.full(n, 1.0 / n, dtype=np.float64)
    if arr.size == n:
        out = arr.copy()
    else:
        old_n = arr.size
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, old_n)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
        out = np.interp(x1, x0, arr)
    return normalize_sequence(out, 1.0)


def hankel_y(p, weights, fp, size, n):
    """
    Given p length n and weights length 2n-1, compute y_i =
    sum_j p_j * weights_{i+j} for i=0..n-1 using one FFT convolution.
    """
    wr = weights[::-1]
    fw = np.fft.rfft(wr, size)
    conv = np.fft.irfft(fp * fw, size)
    start = 2 * n - 2
    stop = n - 2
    return conv[start:stop:-1].copy()


def generate_preview_candidates(P, parent, rng, extra):
    cands = []

    cands.append(np.full(P, 1.0 / float(P), dtype=np.float64))

    x = (np.arange(P) + 0.5) / float(P)

    seqs = []
    if parent is not None:
        seqs.append(parent)
    if extra is not None:
        seqs.append(extra)

    for seq in seqs:
        base = resample_sequence(seq, P)
        cands.append(base)
        for sigma in (0.08, 0.25, 0.60):
            noise = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, P))
            cands.append(normalize_sequence(base * noise, 1.0))

    for gamma in (0.30, 0.50, 0.80, 1.00, 1.50, 2.00, 3.00):
        cands.append(normalize_sequence(x ** gamma, 1.0))
        cands.append(normalize_sequence((1.0 - x) ** gamma, 1.0))
        cands.append(normalize_sequence(
            x ** gamma * (1.0 - x) ** (max(0.1, 0.7 * (1.0 - gamma) + 0.2)), 1.0
        ))

    for a in (0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0, 8.0):
        cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(a * x), 1.0))
        cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.exp(-a * x), 1.0))

    for amp in (0.30, 0.50, 0.70, 0.90):
        phases = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi, 8, endpoint=False)
        for phase in phases:
            f = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase)
            cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f, 0.0), 1.0))

            f = 1.0 + amp * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x + phase) + \
                0.5 * amp * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x + 1.7 * phase)
            cands.append(normalize_sequence(np.maximum(f, 0.0), 1.0))

            f = np.exp(0.5 + amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x + phase) - x)
            cands.append(normalize_sequence(f, 1.0))

    for _ in range(24):
        m = int(rng.integers(4, 17))
        sig = np.zeros(P, dtype=np.float64)
        for k in range(1, m + 1):
            amp = abs(rng.standard_normal()) * 0.8 / (float(k) ** 0.9)
            phase = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            sig += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + phase)
        sig += rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0) * x
        f = np.exp(sig - np.max(sig))
        cands.append(normalize_sequence(f, 1.0))

    if len(cands) > 150:
        cands = cands[:150]
    return cands


def rank_candidates(cands, n, limit, size=None):
    scored = []
    if size is None:
        size = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
    for idx, p in enumerate(cands):
        sc = fast_score_p(p, n, size)
        if np.isfinite(sc):
            scored.append((float(sc), int(idx), p))
    scored.sort()
    out = []
    for _, _, p in scored[:limit]:
        out.append(p)
    if scored:
        return out, scored[0][0]
    return out, float("inf")


def optimize_smooth(p0, deadline, rng, beta_start=3.0, beta_max=420.0,
                    lam_sq=0.8, lam_hinge=1.5, momentum=0.85,
                    step0=0.02, verbose=False):
    """
    Smooth optimization over log-probabilities.

    p is kept on the simplex by softmax.  The direction is a normalized
    gradient of a combined log-sum-exp + variance + hinge loss computed
    in O(n log n).
    """
    n = int(len(p0))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    p = normalize_sequence(p0, 1.0)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_power_of_two(max(8, 3 * n - 2))

    logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    logp -= np.max(logp)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_p(p, n)

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    beta = float(beta_start)
    lam_sq_local = float(lam_sq)
    lam_hinge_local = float(lam_hinge)
    step = float(step0)

    no_improve = 0
    it = 0
    last_adjust = time.monotonic()
    last_print = -100.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = softmax_log(logp)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = normalize_sequence(best_p, 1.0)
            logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
            logp -= np.max(logp)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, size)
        c = c[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        r = n * c
        cur_score = float(2.0 * r.max())

        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            best_score = fast_score_p(best_p, n)
            break

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            step = min(0.08, step * 1.02)
            if verbose and time.monotonic() - last_print > 30.0:
                print(f"    smooth n={n} it={it} score={cur_score:.8f} "
                      f"beta={beta:.1f} step={step:.5f}")
                last_print = time.monotonic()
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        # Periodic adaptation and occasional restart.
        if time.monotonic() - last_adjust > 30.0 and no_improve > 800:
            beta = min(beta * 1.35, float(beta_max))
            if best_score < 1.56:
                lam_hinge_local = min(lam_hinge_local * 1.15, 3.0)
                lam_sq_local = max(0.15, lam_sq_local * 0.85)
            else:
                lam_sq_local = min(lam_sq_local + 0.10, 2.5)
            step = max(0.002, step * 0.9)
            v *= 0.5
            no_improve = 0
            last_adjust = time.monotonic()

        if it > 5000 and no_improve > 12000 and \
                time.monotonic() > last_adjust + 60.0:
            sigma = 0.10 + 0.10 * ((it // 12000) % 3)
            logp = np.log(best_p + 1e-300)
            logp -= np.max(logp)
            logp = logp + rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            step = max(0.015, step)
            no_improve = 0
            last_adjust = time.monotonic()

        rmax = float(r.max())
        if not np.isfinite(rmax):
            break

        z = beta * (r - rmax)
        w = np.exp(z)
        wsum = float(w.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(wsum) or wsum <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(r))] = 1.0
        else:
            w /= wsum

        eps = 0.02
        w_log = (1.0 - eps) * w + eps / float(L)

        rm = r - r.mean()
        rm_max = float(np.abs(rm).max())
        if rm_max > 1e-12:
            w_sq = rm / rm_max
        else:
            w_sq = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

        tau = 0.75
        ex = np.maximum(r - tau, 0.0)
        ex_max = float(ex.max())
        if ex_max > 1e-12:
            w_h = ex / ex_max
        else:
            w_h = np.zeros(L, dtype=np.float64)

        w_comb = w_log + lam_sq_local * w_sq + lam_hinge_local * w_h

        y = hankel_y(p, w_comb, fp, size, n)
        g = n * y
        h = g - g.mean()

        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        hmax = float(np.abs(h).max())
        if hmax <= 1e-12:
            if it > 1000:
                noise = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
                noise -= noise.mean()
                nm = float(np.abs(noise).max())
                if nm > 1e-15:
                    logp = logp + 0.05 * (noise / nm)
                v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
            else:
                break
        else:
            if hmax > 2.0:
                h = h / hmax
            v = momentum * v + h
            vmax = float(np.abs(v).max())
            if vmax > 5.0:
                v = v / vmax
            logp = logp - step * v

        it += 1

    best_p = normalize_sequence(best_p, 1.0)
    final_score = fast_score_p(best_p, n)
    if np.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score

    return best_p, best_score


def polish_max(p0, deadline, rng, peak_count=10, momentum=0.80, step0=0.012):
    """Final steepest-descent-like polish using true active convolution peaks."""
    n = int(len(p0))
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64), float("inf")

    p = normalize_sequence(p0, 1.0)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    size = next_power_of_two(max(8, 2 * n - 1))
    peak_count = min(peak_count, L)

    logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
    logp -= np.max(logp)

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = fast_score_p(p, n)

    v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    step = float(step0)
    no_improve = 0
    last_adjust = time.monotonic()

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        p = softmax_log(logp)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
            p = normalize_sequence(best_p, 1.0)
            logp = np.log(p + 1e-300)
            logp -= np.max(logp)
            v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        fp = np.fft.rfft(p, size)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fp * fp, size)
        c = c[:L]
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)

        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * c.max())
        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            break

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_p = p.copy()
            no_improve = 0
            step = min(0.04, step * 1.01)
        else:
            no_improve += 1

        if time.monotonic() - last_adjust > 40.0 and no_improve > 500:
            step = max(0.001, step * 0.75)
            v *= 0.4
            no_improve = 0
            last_adjust = time.monotonic()

        if peak_count >= L:
            top = np.argsort(c)[::-1][:peak_count]
        else:
            top = np.argpartition(c, L - peak_count)[L - peak_count:]
            top = top[np.argsort(-c[top])]

        G = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kval in top:
            k = int(kval)
            lo = max(0, k - (n - 1))
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            if hi >= lo:
                idxs = np.arange(k - lo, k - hi - 1, -1)
                G[lo:hi + 1] += p[idxs]

        g = n * G
        h = g - g.mean()
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(h)):
            h = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)

        hmax = float(np.abs(h).max())
        if hmax <= 1e-12:
            if no_improve > 1000:
                break
        else:
            if hmax > 2.0:
                h = h / hmax
            v = momentum * v + h
            vmax = float(np.abs(v).max())
            if vmax > 5.0:
                v = v / vmax
            logp = logp - step * v

    best_p = normalize_sequence(best_p, 1.0)
    final_score = fast_score_p(best_p, n)
    if np.isfinite(final_score) and final_score < best_score:
        best_score = final_score

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2350.0, **kwargs):
    """Main search routine invoked by the evaluator."""
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    try:
        raw_budget = float(budget_s)
    except Exception:
        raw_budget = 2350.0
    raw_budget = min(max(60.0, raw_budget), 2380.0)

    total_deadline = time.monotonic() + raw_budget - 25.0

    parent_raw = globals().get("height_sequence_1", None)
    parent = None
    if parent_raw is not None:
        try:
            parent = np.asarray(parent_raw, dtype=np.float64)
            parent = np.nan_to_num(parent, nan=0.0, posinf=0.0, neginf=0.0)
            parent = np.maximum(parent, 0.0)
            if parent.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(parent)):
                parent = None
        except Exception:
            parent = None

    best_p = None
    best_score = float("inf")
    best_n = 0

    # Use multiple lengths.  16384 is usually fine for exact convolution.
    if raw_budget >= 1800.0:
        lengths = [4096, 8192, 16384]
        fracs = [0.26, 0.30, 0.44]
    elif raw_budget >= 1200.0:
        lengths = [4096, 8192]
        fracs = [0.42, 0.58]
    else:
        lengths = [4096]
        fracs = [1.0]

    # Optional: keep parent length in the list if it is reasonable and
    # represents a distinct target.
    if parent is not None and parent.size >= 1500 and parent.size <= 10000:
        pn = int(parent.size)
        if pn not in lengths:
            # Insert before the next larger length.
            inserted = False
            for i, ln in enumerate(lengths):
                if pn < ln:
                    lengths.insert(i, pn)
                    fracs.insert(i, 0.20)
                    inserted = True
                    break
            if not inserted:
                lengths.append(pn)
                fracs.append(0.20)
            # Rescale fractions to sum 1.
            s = float(sum(fracs))
            fracs = [f / s for f in fracs]

    main_start = time.monotonic()
    total_avoid_tail = max(30.0, raw_budget - 25.0)

    preview_len = 1024
    preview_size = next_power_of_two(2 * preview_len - 1)

    cum = 0.0
    sum_fracs = float(sum(fracs))
    for idx_len, n in enumerate(lengths):
        frac = fracs[idx_len] / sum_fracs
        cum += frac
        len_deadline = min(total_deadline, main_start + total_avoid_tail * cum)
        if len_deadline - time.monotonic() < 80.0:
            break

        n = int(n)
        print(f"[length {n}] starting at "
              f"{time.monotonic()-main_start:7.1f}s, deadline fraction={cum:.2f}")

        # Generate and rank preview candidates.
        preview_cands = generate_preview_candidates(preview_len, parent, rng, best_p)
        preview_scored, _ = rank_candidates(
            preview_cands, preview_len, limit=6, size=preview_size
        )

        starts = []
        for pc in preview_scored:
            starts.append(resample_sequence(pc, n))

        if parent is not None:
            starts.append(resample_sequence(parent, n))
        if best_p is not None:
            starts.append(resample_sequence(best_p, n))

        # Rank starting points for this length.
        n_score = next_power_of_two(2 * n - 1)
        start_scored = []
        for i_s, st in enumerate(starts):
            sc = fast_score_p(st, n, n_score)
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                start_scored.append((float(sc), int(i_s), st))
        start_scored.sort()

        if not start_scored:
            start_scored.append((2.0, 0, np.full(n, 1.0 / float(n), dtype=np.float64)))

        # Brief optimization of several starts.
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining_len = max(120.0, len_deadline - now)
        brief_total = min(240.0, remaining_len * 0.30)
        if brief_total < 60.0:
            brief_total = remaining_len * 0.20

        len_best_p = None
        len_best_sc = float("inf")

        # Take the best initial point plus two adapted random/parent starts.
        brief_starts = []
        for _, _, st in start_scored[:3]:
            brief_starts.append(st)

        if len(brief_starts) > 0:
            brief_each = brief_total / float(len(brief_starts))
            for st in brief_starts:
                brief_deadline = time.monotonic() + max(20.0, brief_each)
                beta_st = 3.0
                sc0 = fast_score_p(st, n, n_score)
                if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < 1.58:
                    beta_st = 18.0
                pp, ss = optimize_smooth(
                    st, brief_deadline, rng,
                    beta_start=beta_st,
                    step0=0.018,
                    verbose=False
                )
                if np.isfinite(ss) and ss < len_best_sc:
                    len_best_sc = float(ss)
                    len_best_p = pp

        # Also consider the best initial start without brief text if it wins.
        for sc0, _, st in start_scored:
            if np.isfinite(sc0) and sc0 < len_best_sc:
                len_best_sc = float(sc0)
                len_best_p = st

        if len_best_p is None:
            len_best_p, len_best_sc = start_scored[0][2], start_scored[0][0]

        # Long optimization.
        now = time.monotonic()
        remaining_len = max(60.0, len_deadline - now)
        polish_fraction = 0.18 if remaining_len > 300.0 else 0.10
        long_end = now + remaining_len * (1.0 - polish_fraction)
        long_end = min(long_end, len_deadline - 5.0)

        beta_st = 3.0
        if len_best_sc < 1.58:
            beta_st = 22.0
        elif len_best_sc < 1.68:
            beta_st = 10.0

        long_p, long_sc = optimize_smooth(
            len_best_p, long_end, rng,
            beta_start=beta_st,
            step0=0.02,
            verbose=True
        )
        if long_sc < len_best_sc:
            len_best_sc = long_sc
            len_best_p = long_p

        # Active-peak polish.
        polish_deadline = min(len_deadline, time.monotonic() + local_polish_time)
        pol_p, pol_sc = polish_max(
            len_best_p, polish_deadline, rng,
            peak_count=min(12, max(6, n // 1000)),
            step0=0.012
        )
        if pol_sc < len_best_sc:
            len_best_sc = pol_sc
            len_best_p = pol_p

        # Global update.
        if np.isfinite(len_best_sc) and len_best_sc < best_score:
            best_score = len_best_sc
            best_p = len_best_p.copy()
            best_n = n
            print(f"[length {n}] new best fast score={best_score:.8f}")

            # Optional exact validation when cheap enough and time remains.
            if n <= 12000 and time.monotonic() + 90.0 < total_deadline:
                try:
                    exact_list = [float(x) for x in best_p]
                    exact_sc = float(evaluate_sequence(exact_list))
                    if np.isfinite(exact_sc):
                        print(f"[length {n}] exact validated score={exact_sc:.8f}")
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Fallback.
    if best_p is None or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_p)):
        n_fb = 4096
        best_p = np.full(n_fb, 1.0 / float(n_fb), dtype=np.float64)
        best_n = n_fb
        best_score = 2.0

    # Final normalization and sanity check.
    best_p = normalize_sequence(best_p, 1.0)
    if best_p.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(best_p)):
        n_fb = 1024
        best_p = np.full(n_fb, 1.0 / float(n_fb), dtype=np.float64)

    # Final optional exact validation for the returned sequence, only when safe.
    if best_p.size <= 10000 and time.monotonic() + 80.0 < total_deadline:
        try:
            out_list = [float(x) for x in best_p]
            final_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
            if np.isfinite(final_exact):
                print(f"[final] exact score={final_exact:.8f}, n={best_p.size}")
        except Exception:
            pass

    result = [float(x) for x in best_p]
    # Ensure no invalid values.
    cleaned = []
    for x in result:
        if not np.isfinite(x) or x < 0.0:
            cleaned.append(0.0)
        else:
            cleaned.append(x)
    return cleaned
```
